# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccessMode

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessMode.json), input validation.

Type: ["read-only","ask","auto-review","full-access"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"ask"
```

## AccessModeOffer

[JSON Schema](schema/AccessModeOffer.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| mode | yes | [AccessMode.json](schema/AccessMode.json) |  |
| native | yes | [PermissionMode.json](schema/PermissionMode.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "mode": "read-only",
  "native": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, no emoji, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"AB"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| resetCredits | no | [QuotaResetCredits.json](schema/QuotaResetCredits.json) |  |
| usageNotice | no | object | {"properties":{"reason":{"type":"string","enum":["signed_out","unavailable"]},"message":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256},"observedAt":{"type":"number","minimum":0}},"required":["reason","message","observedAt"]} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"homeUnavailable":{"type":"string","enum":["home_missing","home_unreadable","foreign_home","home_conflict"]}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_in",
  "billingMode": "api",
  "observedAt": 7,
  "resetCredits": {
    "available": 6
  },
  "usage": {
    "inputTokens": 7
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | [AcpHomeStrategy.json](schema/AcpHomeStrategy.json) |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| antigravitySignIn | no | [AntigravitySignInMethod.json](schema/AntigravitySignInMethod.json) |  |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "antigravitySignIn": "enterprise",
  "availability": "near_limit",
  "badgeUsesInitial": true,
  "homeStrategy": "managed",
  "id": "example",
  "implicit": true,
  "isDefault": false,
  "label": "example",
  "provider": "cursor",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "unknown",
    "billingMode": "unknown",
    "blockers": {
      "limitError": {
        "resetsAt": 6,
        "usedPercent": 6,
        "windowDurationMins": 4
      }
    },
    "observedAt": 0,
    "usage": {
      "inputTokens": 2
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "signedInAs": "example"
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": false
  },
  "coverage": "legacy",
  "mcp": "http",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": false,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": true
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

### Variant 11

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "review-on-studio",
  "role": "Review-0.9-build",
  "task": "Review the 0.9 build and list failing benchmarks"
}
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.link_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

```json

  "op": "thread.link_pr",
  "repo": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/AgentItem.json), input validation.

### html

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| runId | no | [RunId.json](schema/RunId.json) |  |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| nativeId | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| executionSource | no | [ExecutionSource.json](schema/ExecutionSource.json) |  |
| complete | yes | boolean |  |
| inherited | no | [InheritedItem.json](schema/InheritedItem.json) |  |
| type | yes | `"html"` |  |
| visualization | yes | [HtmlVisualization.json](schema/HtmlVisualization.json) |  |
| raw | no | array | {"default":[],"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/RawPayload.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "complete": false,
  "createdAt": 5,
  "executionSource": {
    "nativeSessionId": "example",
    "selection": {
      "instanceId": "example",
      "provider": "cursor"
    }
  },
  "id": "example",
  "nativeId": "example",
  "raw": [],
  "runId": "example",
  "type": "html",
  "visualization": {
    "document": {
      "bytes": 3,
      "delivery": "native_pdf",
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| blocked | no | [BrowserOriginBlock.json](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json) |  |

## CatalogList

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId, draft or workspace is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| draft | no | object | {"properties":{"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["draftId","workspaceId","provider"]} |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "draft": {
    "draftId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "provider": "codex",
    "workspaceId": "example"
  },
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "catalog.list"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/CommandsList.json), input validation.

| type | yes | `"commands.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

```json
    "draftId": "example",

  },
  "requestId": "example",
  "type": "commands.list"
}
```

Semantic rule: seq must equal view.seq; a thread view's itemSeqs holds the creation sequence of every item in itemOrder.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |

```json
  "subscriptionId": "example",

    "seq": 7,

    "window": {
      "before": null,
      "counts": [],
      "total": 9
    }
  }
}
```

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| type | yes | `"events"` |  |
| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |
| afterSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

```json
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 9,
  "type": "events"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/CursorAuthEvent.json), input validation.

### cursor.auth.login

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"cursor.auth.login"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| loginId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| instanceId | yes | [AccountId.json](schema/AccountId.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["starting","browser","complete","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| expiresAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |
| auth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| error | no | ["login_failed","expired","cancelled"] |  |

```json
}
```

## DelegateTaskOperation

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegateTaskOperation.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Either one task (requestId, task, role, provider) or tasks: [{ requestId, task, role, provider }, ...].

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"delegate_task"` |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| access | no | [AccessRequest.json](schema/AccessRequest.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| effort | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","const":"inherit"},{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AgentEffort.json"}]} |
| options | no | [AgentServiceOptions.json](schema/AgentServiceOptions.json) |  |
| requestId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| task | no | [MessageText.json](schema/MessageText.json) |  |
| role | no | [DelegationRole.json](schema/DelegationRole.json) |  |
| wait | no | boolean |  |
| waitMs | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":600000} |
| workspace | no | [AgentWorkspace.json](schema/AgentWorkspace.json) |  |
| estimatedLoad | no | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
| machine | no | [MachineName.json](schema/MachineName.json) |  |
| project | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tasks | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DelegationTask.json"}} |
| await | no | ["none","any","all"] |  |
| timeoutSeconds | no | [WaitTimeoutSeconds.json](schema/WaitTimeoutSeconds.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "access": "read-only",
  "effort": "high",
  "machine": "studio",
  "op": "delegate_task",
  "project": "relay",
  "provider": "claude",
  "requestId": "review-on-studio",
  "role": "Review-0.9-build",
  "task": "Review the 0.9 build and list failing benchmarks"
}
```

## DeviceServerMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceServerMessage.json), input validation.

### devices.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| data | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | [DeviceFailure.json](schema/DeviceFailure.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "ok": true,
  "requestId": "kCPP_aZvXRY97i88NjJ6parqVE6VF2-baoUoO",
  "type": "devices.result"
}
```

### devices.state

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [DeviceState.json](schema/DeviceState.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "state": {
    "booting": false,
    "controller": "human",
    "device": {
      "id": "android:_jMbgjbUVDOxlc-QtVbcWKG1E.wy8aZJCQsiqzx4wBzgkaSv5hwtJi2hELsXXU27OYW5.k1pOXKr",
      "name": "example",
      "platform": "android",
      "runtime": "example",
      "state": "booted"
    },
    "enabled": false,
    "lifecycle": "live"
  },
  "type": "devices.state"
}
```

### devices.enabled

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.enabled"` |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "enabled": false,
  "type": "devices.enabled"
}
```

### devices.inventory

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.inventory"` |  |
| devices | yes | array | {"maxItems":1024,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AppDevice.json"}} |
| issues | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":2,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeviceFailure.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "devices": [],
  "issues": [],
  "type": "devices.inventory"
}
```

### devices.logs

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"devices.logs"` |  |
| deviceId | yes | [AppDeviceId.json](schema/AppDeviceId.json) |  |

```json
  "deviceId": "android:xU3YMDLcCgtn.M4EGZBealuzIOt.K7_3v.-SBaF2p.bjPnEZS-j8Cs56fz5a0SH",

  "lines": [],
  "sequence": 2,
  "type": "devices.logs"
```

## DeviceSettings

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceSettings.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| appearance | no | ["light","dark"] |  |
| location | no | object | {"properties":{"latitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-90,"maximum":90},"longitude":{"type":"number","minimum":-180,"maximum":180}},"required":["latitude","longitude"]} |
| locale | no | string | {"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z]{2,8}(?:[-_][a-zA-Z0-9]{2,8}){0,2}$"} |

```json
    "latitude": 1,
    "longitude": 1
  }
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/DeviceState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| device | yes | [AppDevice.json](schema/AppDevice.json) |  |
| enabled | yes | boolean |  |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| lifecycle | yes | ["idle","starting","live","stopping","failed"] |  |
| booting | no | boolean |  |
| cleanupRequired | no | boolean |  |
| recordingNotice | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| recording | no | boolean |  |
| streamId | no | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |
| touch | no | ["multi","pointer","gestures"] |  |
| controller | yes | ["none","human","agent"] |  |
| ownedHere | no | boolean |  |

| leaseExpiresAt | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| error | no | [DeviceFailure.json](schema/DeviceFailure.json) |  |
| activity | no | [DeviceActivity.json](schema/DeviceActivity.json) |  |

```json
  "booting": false,
  "controller": "agent",

    "id": "android:fpGkYMyjKDtvn1jsrnxIGRozT60jeMmvNznkeg1BZ--reCKUktNZ-xbXftQ2C0ealAPvv21PmINzWIImCuUym6fGxAwGi-KvHTy555yN117vTbAciDiLvq1Lg7UjYlXgBTpRxK4-ugt7oOJ.v6Ht.Nh5Gip60qVE04nlWCjy6j5hCZnjo4UfbCtm",
```

## InteractionRepeat

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
  "metrics": {
    "captureOverheadPct": {
      "median": 6,
      "worst": 7
    },
    "frames": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 0
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 1,
      "worst": 0
    },
    "hostLoad": {
      "median": 1,
      "worst": 2
    },
    "latencyMs": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 9
    },
    "longTaskMs": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 4
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 7
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 5,
      "worst": 3
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "high",
      "verdict": "inconclusive"
    }
  ]
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| wholeTurns | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 5,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "turnOrdinal": 6,
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

## ItemsWindowResponse

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowResponse.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: itemSeqs holds the creation sequence of every item.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| omitted | no | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OmittedItem.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "itemSeqs": {},
  "items": [],
  "itemsAfter": null,
  "itemsBefore": 7,
  "requestId": "example",
  "seq": 7,
  "targetSeq": null,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| status | yes | [NotificationStatus.json](schema/NotificationStatus.json) |  |

| preview | no | string | {"maxLength":300} |

[JSON Schema](schema/OffshiftCriteria.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Criterion IDs must be unique.

```json

  {

    "id": "00KrV",
    "independent": true,

    ]
  }
```

## OffshiftCriterion

[JSON Schema](schema/OffshiftCriterion.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$","description":"A stable id for the criterion (letters, digits, _ . -)."} |
| description | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"The observable behaviour that must hold."} |
| requiredKinds | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftEvidenceKind.json"},"description":"Evidence kinds that must each have a current pass."} |
| independent | no | boolean | {"description":"Passes count only from another provider or model than the run owner's."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "description": "example",
  "id": "IvalfbHOo_D",
  "requiredKinds": [
    "ui"
  ]
}
```

## OffshiftEvidence

[JSON Schema](schema/OffshiftEvidence.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| criterionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$","description":"The criterion this observation checks."} |
| kind | yes | ["automated","source","ui","human","manual"] | {"description":"Which required kind this observation satisfies."} |
| result | yes | ["pass","fail"] | {"description":"What the check observed."} |
| revision | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512,"description":"The revision from get, read before the check ran."} |
| action | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"The command or interaction actually performed."} |
| artifact | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"Where the full output is kept: a path, URL or report reference."} |
| note | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"The observed result and environment, in a sentence or two."} |
| recordedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| by | no | union | {"default":null,"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftAuthor.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "example",
  "artifact": "example",
  "criterionId": "YxT7ZOz",
  "kind": "ui",
  "note": "example",
  "recordedAt": 0,
  "result": "fail",
  "revision": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/OffshiftEvidenceInput.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| criterionId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$","description":"The criterion this observation checks."} |
| kind | yes | ["automated","source","ui","human","manual"] | {"description":"Which required kind this observation satisfies."} |
| result | yes | ["pass","fail"] | {"description":"What the check observed."} |
| revision | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512,"description":"The revision from get, read before the check ran."} |
| action | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"The command or interaction actually performed."} |
| artifact | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"Where the full output is kept: a path, URL or report reference."} |
| note | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"description":"The observed result and environment, in a sentence or two."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "example",
  "artifact": "example",
  "criterionId": "8.kC40Ru",
  "kind": "human",
  "note": "example",
  "result": "pass",
  "revision": "example"
}
```

## OffshiftRun

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| outcome | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| endpoint | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| criteria | yes | [OffshiftCriteria.json](schema/OffshiftCriteria.json) |  |
| owner | no | union | {"default":null,"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftAuthor.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| playbook | no | [OffshiftPlaybookId.json](schema/OffshiftPlaybookId.json) |  |
| steps | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":24,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftStep.json"}} |
| phase | yes | [OffshiftPhase.json](schema/OffshiftPhase.json) |  |
| version | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| blocker | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096},{"type":"null"}]} |
| note | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| evidence | yes | array | {"maxItems":320,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftEvidence.json"}} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

```json
  "createdAt": 9,
  "criteria": [
    {
      "description": "example",
      "id": "kRc2ATfX_",
      "requiredKinds": [
        "source"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "endpoint": "example",
  "evidence": [],
  "id": "6",
  "note": "example",
  "outcome": "example",
  "phase": "plan",
  "threadId": "example",
  "updatedAt": 7,
  "version": 1
}
```

## OffshiftRunCard

[JSON Schema](schema/OffshiftRunCard.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| outcome | yes | string | {"maxLength":512} |
| outcomeTruncated | yes | boolean |  |
| endpoint | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| phase | yes | [OffshiftPhase.json](schema/OffshiftPhase.json) |  |
| blocker | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":512},{"type":"null"}]} |
| version | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [OffshiftRevision.json](schema/OffshiftRevision.json) |  |
| criteria | yes | array | {"maxItems":16,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftCardCriterion.json"}} |
| criteriaCount | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":64} |
| pending | yes | array | {"maxItems":16,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftPending.json"}} |
| pendingCount | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":320} |
| steps | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":24,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"},"title":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":160},"state":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/OffshiftStepState.json"},"reason":{"type":"string","maxLength":160}},"required":["id","title","state"]}} |
| playbook | no | [OffshiftPlaybookId.json](schema/OffshiftPlaybookId.json) |  |
| next | no | string | {"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "blocker": "example",
  "criteria": [],
  "criteriaCount": 1,
  "endpoint": "example",
  "id": "Mmc",
  "next": "example",
  "outcome": "example",
  "outcomeTruncated": true,
  "pending": [],
  "pendingCount": 9,
  "phase": "implement",
  "revision": "example",
  "updatedAt": 8,
  "version": 6
}
```

| result | yes | union | {"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"dismissed":{"type":"boolean"},"providers":{"maxItems":16,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderStatus.json"}},"ready":{"maxItems":16,"type":"array","items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"}},"next":{"type":"object","properties":{"action":{"type":"string","enum":["start_thread","sign_in","install","refresh","configure"]},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"}},"required":["action"]}},"required":["ok","dismissed","providers","ready","next"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"ok":{"type":"boolean","const":false},"error":{"type":"string","enum":["forbidden","unavailable"]}},"required":["ok","error"]}]} |

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | ["recent-complete-items","conversation-first"] |  |
| sources | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffModel.json"}} |
| since | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"handoff_read","type":"string","const":"handoff_read"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 6,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "tool": "handoff_read",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 0,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "since": 5,
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 6,
  "version": 1
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/PreviewLaunch.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Choose exactly one executable, or an attach URL without an executable or autoPort.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| command | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

| args | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096}} |
| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |
| autoPort | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":4096,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "command": "node",
  "name": "example"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"preview.suggestions"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| suggestions | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/PreviewSuggestion.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "suggestions": [],
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "preview.suggestions"
}
```

## Project

[JSON Schema](schema/Project.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| name | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| defaultIcon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| badge | no | [ProjectBadgeStyle.json](schema/ProjectBadgeStyle.json) |  |
| defaults | no | [ProjectDefaults.json](schema/ProjectDefaults.json) |  |
| notes | no | array | {"maxItems":50,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectNote.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "badge": {
    "color": "neutral"
  },
  "id": "example",
  "name": "example",
  "path": "example"
}
```

## ProjectBadgeStyle

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectBadgeStyle.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| color | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| glyph | no | [ProjectGlyph.json](schema/ProjectGlyph.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "glyph": "globe"
}
```

## ProjectCloneUrl

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCloneUrl.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: GitHub owner/repo shorthand, HTTPS, SSH or scp-style git@ URL with a host and repository path; no passwords, HTTPS usernames, whitespace or control bytes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"ssh://git@example.invalid/repo.git"
```

## ProjectCommand

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCommand.json), input validation.

### workspace.publish

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.publish"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| input | yes | [RepositoryPublishInput.json](schema/RepositoryPublishInput.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "input": {
    "account": "W",
    "description": "example",
    "host": "irioi3-ldfd28t-rj33.p8.ju-b91s",
    "name": "N",
    "owner": "jHvP6qJTT",
    "visibility": "private"
  },
  "type": "workspace.publish",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

### workspace.add

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.add"` |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | no | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| create | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "create": true,
  "path": "example",
  "type": "workspace.add"
}
```

### workspace.root.add

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.root.add"` |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| create | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "path": "example",
  "type": "workspace.root.add"
}
```

### workspace.create

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.create"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| git | no | object | {"properties":{"initialBranch":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":1024}}} |
| gitignore | no | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
{
  "git": {},
  "gitignore": "example",
  "name": "|!",
  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.create"
}
```

### workspace.clone

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.clone"` |  |
| parent | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| url | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "name": "((&, (\"&",
  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.clone",
  "url": "example"
}
```

### workspace.update

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.update"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| badge | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectBadgeStyle.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| defaults | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectDefaults.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| notes | no | array | {"maxItems":50,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectNoteEdit.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "icon": "data:image/webp;base64,Q153k7dKTVoWBaU8UKHorMTSdMtHWIW6",
  "name": ",}z+!*)~{#",
  "type": "workspace.update",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.remove"` |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |

## ProjectDefaults

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDefaults.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: `model` is present only together with `provider`.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| worktree | no | boolean |  |
| access | no | [AccessMode.json](schema/AccessMode.json) |  |
| baseBranch | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |
| browserProfileScope | no | [BrowserProfileScope.json](schema/BrowserProfileScope.json) |  |
| checkCommand | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":1024} |

Example:

```json
{
  "browserProfileScope": "global",
  "checkCommand": "example",
  "provider": "claude",
  "worktree": true
}
```

## ProjectDirectoryName

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: A literal existing directory name; no forward slash or NUL. Host filesystem rules apply to backslashes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

## ProjectFolderMatch

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectFolderMatch.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | [ProjectDirectoryName.json](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json) |  |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| isGitRepo | yes | boolean |  |
| isProject | yes | boolean |  |
| lastOpened | no | number | {"minimum":0} |
| recentScore | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| score | yes | number |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "isGitRepo": false,
  "isProject": false,
  "name": "example",
  "path": "example",
  "recentScore": 4,
  "score": 3
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectGit.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| root | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| branch | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},{"type":"null"}]} |
| defaultBranch | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| remotes | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"fetchUrls":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}},"pushUrls":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":4096}}},"required":["name","fetchUrls","pushUrls"]}} |

Example:

```json

  "defaultBranch": "example",
  "remotes": [],
  "root": "example"
}
```

## ProjectGlyph

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectGlyph.json), input validation.

Type: ["code","terminal","globe","database","cloud","robot","rocket","flask","book","cube","game","mobile","palette","gear","lightning","leaf","star","heart"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"mobile"
```

## ProjectIcon

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Semantic rule: Absolute HTTP or HTTPS WHATWG URL, without ASCII tabs, CR, LF or trailing whitespace; at most 4096 UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce URL parsing in application code.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"HTTPS://例え.テスト/icon.png"
```

### Variant 2

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"data:image/png;base64,yRI6vdqJ5d1JejUOcc=="
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| path | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| defaultIcon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| git | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectGit.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| gitUnavailable | no | `"git_quarantined"` |  |
| suggestedRepoRoot | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

Example:

```json
  "defaultIcon": "HTTPS://例え.テスト/icon.png",
  "git": null,
  "path": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One folder name, without separators, traversal or control characters.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"&-{&{)..{"
```

## PromptArgument

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptArgument.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An argument default must have the declared type.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | ["string","number","boolean"] |  |
| required | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| default | no | [PromptValue.json](schema/PromptValue.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "type": "boolean"
}
```

## PromptArguments

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptArguments.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: At most 64 arguments.

Type: object. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
{}
```

## PromptFile

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptFile.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| name | yes | [PromptFileName.json](schema/PromptFileName.json) |  |
| title | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| description | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| scope | yes | [PromptFileScope.json](schema/PromptFileScope.json) |  |
| diagnostics | yes | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CommandDiagnostic.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "description": "example",
  "diagnostics": [],
  "name": "nG.md",
  "scope": {
    "kind": "global"
  },
  "title": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptFileName.json), input validation.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"jE.md"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/PromptFileOperation.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"list"` |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |

```json

  "op": "list"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"read"` |  |
| scope | yes | [PromptFileScope.json](schema/PromptFileScope.json) |  |
| name | yes | [PromptFileName.json](schema/PromptFileName.json) |  |

Example:

```json

  "name": "En.md",
  "op": "read",
  "scope": {
    "kind": "global"
  }
}
```

### Variant 3

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"write"` |  |
| scope | yes | [PromptFileScope.json](schema/PromptFileScope.json) |  |
| name | yes | [PromptFileName.json](schema/PromptFileName.json) |  |

```json
  "name": "qV5fcL0.md",
  "op": "write",
  "scope": {
    "kind": "global"
  },
  "text": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |
| maxCheckpointMb | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":256} |

```json
  "customModels": [],
  "defaultModel": "~|}}z|",
  "enabled": false,
  "favourites": [],
  "hiddenModels": [],
  "hideDeprecated": false,
  "provider": "antigravity"
}
```

```json
      "sourceUrl": "https://example.invalid/",
      "status": "sign_in",
      "verify": {
        "args": [],
        "command": "example",
        "display": "example"
      }
    }
  },
  "type": "provider.install.result"
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references and id equal to instanceId, with either the default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment, or the managed strategy, managed: true and the selectors of the agent's verified profile.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | [AcpHomeStrategy.json](schema/AcpHomeStrategy.json) |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| badgeIcon | no | [AccountBadgeIcon.json](schema/AccountBadgeIcon.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

```json
"antigravity"
```

## ProviderLoginProgress

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderLoginProgress.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| session | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| action | yes | ["login","logout"] |  |
| method | no | ["login","api_key"] |  |
| state | yes | ["starting","awaiting_browser","awaiting_code_entry","awaiting_input","awaiting_api_key","verifying","succeeded","failed","cancelled"] |  |
| expiresAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."},{"type":"string","pattern":"^(?!.*\\s)https:\\/\\/\\S+$"}]} |
| userCode | no | string | {"pattern":"^[A-Z0-9]{4,5}-[A-Z0-9]{4,5}$"} |
| prompt | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| choices | no | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[a-z0-9_-]{1,64}$"},"label":{"type":"string","maxLength":128}},"required":["id","label"],"additionalProperties":false}} |
| message | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| codeEntry | no | `true` |  |
| returnUrlEntry | no | `true` |  |
| hint | no | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| manual | no | object | {"properties":{"action":{"type":"string","const":"open_terminal"},"terminalId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"command":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"instruction":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},"instance":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountInstanceId.json"}},"required":["action","command","instruction"],"additionalProperties":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "login",
  "choices": [],
  "codeEntry": true,
  "expiresAt": 5,
  "manual": {
    "action": "open_terminal",
    "command": "example",
    "instance": "example",
    "instruction": "example"
  },
  "message": "example",
  "prompt": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "sequence": 5,
  "session": "example",
  "state": "starting",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

```json
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ]
}
```

```json
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |
| uiTree | yes | boolean |  |
| semanticActions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["press","focus","setValue","scroll","expand","select","performSecondaryAction","selectText"]}} |
| codecs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["jpeg","h264"]}} |
| permissions | yes | object | {"properties":{"screen":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]},"input":{"type":"string","enum":["granted","denied","prompt","n/a"]}},"required":["screen","input"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capture": {
    "changeDriven": false,
    "displays": false,
    "windows": false
  },
  "codecs": [
    "h264"
  ],
  "input": {
    "keyboard": true,
    "pointer": false,
    "scroll": false,
    "text": false
  },
  "maxSessions": 3,
  "permissionEvents": true,
  "permissions": {
    "input": "denied",
    "screen": "denied"
  },
  "platform": "linux-x11",
  "semanticActions": [],
  "uiTree": false,
  "version": 2
}
```

## ScreenClientMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenClientMessage.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"screen.request"` |  |
| requestId | yes | [ScreenId.json](schema/ScreenId.json) |  |

```json

  "operation": {
    "op": "status"

}
```

## ScreenEndpoint

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenEndpoint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute unix: path without NUL, CR or LF, or an ace-screen named pipe.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"unix:/example/frames.sock"
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| code | yes | ["forbidden","permission_denied","screen_disabled","denied","target_gone","not_supported","bounds","busy","timeout","internal","target_busy","foreground_required","focus_changed","window_minimized","window_offscreen","secure_input_required","clipboard_changed","window_ambiguous","key_unsupported","modifier_unsupported","no_key_window","delivery_unconfirmed"] |  |
| message | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| phase | no | ["rejected-before-dispatch","dispatched","partial"] |  |
| candidates | no | array | {"maxItems":2048,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"windowId":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"bundleId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenBundle.json"},"title":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},"bounds":{"type":"object","properties":{"x":{"type":"number"},"y":{"type":"number"},"w":{"type":"number"},"h":{"type":"number"}},"required":["x","y","w","h"]}},"required":["windowId","title"]}} |

```json
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":10,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "agentId": "example",
  "status": "done"
}
```

## SourceControlPreferences

[JSON Schema](schema/SourceControlPreferences.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Hosts are unique and the selected host must be configured

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hosts | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"host":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubHost.json"},"account":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/GitHubAccount.json"}},"required":["host"]}} |
| host | yes | [GitHubHost.json](schema/GitHubHost.json) |  |
| remote | yes | string | {"maxLength":100,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._-]*$"} |

Example:

```json
{
  "host": "github.com",
  "hosts": [
    {
      "host": "github.com"
    }
  ],
  "remote": "origin"
}
```

| api | yes | ["ready","login_required","account_mismatch","offline","rate_limited","unavailable"] |  |

```json
      }

  },
  "transport": "ready",

  "version": "example"
}
```

| artifacts | no | union | {"default":30,"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":36500},{"type":"null"}]} |
| logs | no | union | {"default":14,"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":36500},{"type":"null"}]} |
| worktrees | no | union | {"default":30,"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":36500},{"type":"null"}]} |
| caches | no | union | {"default":7,"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":1,"maximum":36500},{"type":"null"}]} |
| idleThreads | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "threads": null
}
```

## Thread

[JSON Schema](schema/Thread.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| listOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place in the Home list when it is ordered by hand (threads.sortBy manual), higher first. Absent: the thread's creation time stands in, so a new thread leads."} |

| readAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

| autoSettle | no | `false` |  |
| settleWhenDone | no | `true` |  |
| prSettled | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| activitySeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| hasSentMessage | no | boolean |  |
| details | no | [ThreadDetails.json](schema/ThreadDetails.json) |  |
| live | no | [ThreadRunMetadata.json](schema/ThreadRunMetadata.json) |  |
| id | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| title | yes | string |  |
| queue | no | [QueueState.json](schema/QueueState.json) |  |
| run | no | [OffshiftRunMark.json](schema/OffshiftRunMark.json) |  |
| goal | no | [ProviderGoal.json](schema/ProviderGoal.json) |  |
| titleSource | no | ["provisional","provider","generated","person","agent"] |  |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| backend | no | ["acp","cursor-sdk"] |  |
| continuation | no | object | {"properties":{"state":{"type":"string","const":"read_only"},"reason":{"type":"string","const":"cursor_cli_retired"},"actionId":{"type":"string","const":"thread.continue_new"}},"required":["state","reason","actionId"]} |
| capabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |
| permission | no | [PermissionState.json](schema/PermissionState.json) |  |
| handoff | no | object | {"properties":{"sourceThreadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"},"bytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["sourceThreadId","truncated","bytes"]} |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| effectiveCapabilities | no | [Capabilities.json](schema/Capabilities.json) |  |

| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| updatedAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| archivedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| imported | no | [ImportedProvenance.json](schema/ImportedProvenance.json) |  |
| lineage | no | [ThreadLineage.json](schema/ThreadLineage.json) |  |

| sinceTime | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

```json
  "sinceTime": 2,
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "thread.catchUp"
}
```

| truncated | yes | boolean |  |

```json
  "approvalsAnswered": 8,
  "approvalsAsked": 7,
  "approvalsAutoReviewed": 7,
  "approvalsPending": 5,
  "commands": [],
  "commandsFailed": 9,
  "commandsRun": 5,
  "errors": 9,
  "files": [],
  "inputTokens": 2,
  "outputTokens": 1,
  "subagentsFinished": 0,
  "subagentsStarted": 7,
  "toolCounts": {},
  "truncated": true
}
```

| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

| before | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| after | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

```json

  "limit": 4,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "turns.page"
```
```json
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

## UsageResult

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cursor | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| omittedEvents | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| timezone | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| priceVersion | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| priceAsOf | no | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| priceSources | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}} |
| costLabel | no | `"estimate"` |  |
| tokenSource | no | `"cli"` |  |
| retainedFrom | no | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| accounts | no | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountSummary.json"}} |
| rows | yes | array | {"maxItems":1000,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageRow.json"}} |

| burn | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageBurn.json"}} |

```json
  "costLabel": "estimate",
  "cursor": 1,
  "omittedEvents": 1,
  "priceAsOf": "4824-02-29",
  "priceVersion": "example",
  "rows": [],
```

## UsageUpdated

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageUpdated.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: If usageScope is provider_session or model_session, counterKey is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| usageScope | no | ["agent","provider_session","model_session"] |  |
| counterKey | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| contextTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextSessionId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| reasoningTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWriteTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cacheWrite1hTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1} |
| accountId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| counterMode | no | ["cumulative","incremental"] |  |
| type | yes | `"usage.updated"` |  |
| agentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| inputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outputTokens | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| contextWindow | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| costUsd | no | number | {"minimum":0} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "costUsd": 2,
  "counterMode": "cumulative",
  "inputTokens": 1,
  "outputTokens": 5,
  "type": "usage.updated"
}
```

```json
  "type": "workspace.result"
}
```

| percent | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |
